| PERF-15 | Peak journal during an import: **≤ 5% of the snapshot's size** | relative: 0.0–0.1 MB for 1M rows [E-3], measured on **one `file_obs`-shaped table only**; the `name` and `folder_path` `UNIQUE` indexes modify existing pages once the Library holds other data, and those pages are journalled (G0-O03) | C4, with the full schema on a Library that already holds other sources (TEST-P1) |
| PERF-16 | Cancelling the saving phase at 2M files returns, rolled back, **within 2 s** | projected: appended pages are not journalled; the journalled dictionary pages (G0-O03) are not yet measured | C5 |

`c0c031f`'s PERF-09 ("a real-world Library grows past 20 GB") could not be observed without telemetry (INV-05); it is
replaced by the local triggers of §15.5.

### 15.4 Benchmark plan and gates

- **C4, engine (TEST-P1):** one-transaction imports of 1M, 2M and 10M synthetic rows in key order with the shipped
  engine on Windows, using the **full schema with dictionary interning** (`name`, `folder_path`) into a Library that
  **already holds other snapshots and sources** (G0-O03): duration, peak journal, peak working set, database growth; deletion of a 2M snapshot; behaviour
  when the Library's volume fills (`SQLITE_FULL`, rollback, no damage). Answers Q-17. **Stop condition:** PERF-01,
  PERF-14's engine share or PERF-15 missed by more than 50% → C4 stops and §11.8 is amended by review (the prepared
  alternative is batched import into a hidden staging state, which would bring back the recovery rules of `c0c031f`
  and must be re-reviewed).
- **C5, capture (TEST-P2):** the existing 10k, 60k and 250k synthetic trees, and generated 1M and 2M trees: scan without
  saving; scan with saving (reports-ready time, saving time, spool size, bytes per observation, peak memory);
  traversal order through the spool; one real system drive, reporting aggregates only (never names).
- **C10, scale (TEST-P3):** Libraries with 1, 10 and 50 snapshots of a 1M-file source; comparing latest with previous
  and latest with oldest; timeline, search, top-N; deletion; CAP-03's churn test; opening the Library.
- **Method:** a fresh process per run, warm cache (as `docs/benchmarks/native.md`), `PeakWorkingSet64` read in process,
  the Library on the same SSD.
- **Output:** `docs/benchmarks/library.md`, with measured values, the method and caveats only (no extrapolated claims),
  in the style of v1.

### 15.5 Capacity reassessment triggers (CAP-01 to CAP-05)

Each trigger is observable locally, without telemetry.

| ID | Trigger | Consequence |
|---|---|---|
| CAP-01 | A traversal-order capture measured in C5 or C10 costs more than 90 B per file observation | Reassess delta or deduplicated storage before release |
| CAP-02 | A Library exceeds 10% of its volume's capacity or 20 GB | The app shows a warning on Home and in Settings with the size per source; maintainers reassess when users report it |
| CAP-03 | C10's churn test (20 cycles of deleting the oldest and importing a new 1M-file snapshot on a 10-snapshot Library) leaves the file larger than 1.5× its live data, or slows comparison by more than 25% | Compact (SREQ-09) becomes a 1.1.x priority, with its temporary-storage review |
| CAP-04 | Import slower than 60 s, or comparison slower than 10 s, at 2M files on the reference machine | Reassess indexes and the storage model |
| CAP-05 | A measured 50-snapshot Library deviates from §15.2's projection by more than 20% | §15.2 is corrected before any size claim is documented |

---

## 16. Security and privacy threat model

### 16.1 Zones

| Zone | Trust | Write policy |
|---|---|---|
| **Z1: the tree being observed** | The user's data | **Read-only during observation, always** (INV-01, INV-02, INV-17). Metadata-only listing; no handles to files, only 0-access handles to directories. StorageInventory never writes a user file. If Z3 lies inside a source (RX-01), that directory is written only **outside** every observation window |
| **Z2: the report folder** | Chosen by the user; outside Z1 (v1) | Create-new only (v1), plus the observation spool: create-new, exclusive, delete-on-close, one of the run's own names (SPOOL-03, SPOOL-04) |
| **Z3: the Library** | Owned by StorageInventory; **contains private data** | Transactional writes to the owned set only (LIB-02), each inside a mutation lease of the interlock, so never overlapping an observation window in either order (INV-17, OBS-07); never on a network or sync location; one rename (set-aside) on explicit action, under the writer lock |

§2.2 states the four relaxations (RX-01 to RX-04) and their narrower replacement rules; this table and §2 say the
same thing.

### 16.2 New capabilities inventory (everything v1.1 adds that can write, delete, launch or call native code)

| Capability | Where (only) | Zone | Guard |
|---|---|---|---|
| Create the Library directory | `LibraryStore.EnsureLibraryFolder` | Z3 | Validated Library path (§6.4); never during observation |
| Create `library.lock`; hold it with `FileShare.None` | `LibraryStore.CreateOrOpenWriterLock`, held by `LibrarySession` | Z3 | Open first, CreateNew if absent, one re-open after a lost race (LIB-07 step 3); always before any other member is inspected; the directory is re-checked for reparse points first; held until the process exits |
| Create the database file | `LibraryStore.CreateEmptyDatabase` (`FileMode.CreateNew`) | Z3 | Never overwrites; SQLite never creates the main file (SEC-06) |
| Read the database header before SQLite | `LibraryStore.ReadHeader` (`FileMode.Open`, `FileAccess.Read`) | Z3 | 100 bytes; refuses foreign, WAL-format, newer files (SEC-32) |
| Open or write the database | `LibraryDatabase` (`SqliteConnection`, `Mode` explicit, `Pooling=False`) | Z3 | Checks at open (§6.7); SQL constants only (SEC-04) |
| SQLite rollback journal (`-journal`) | The SQLite engine, natively | Z3 | Created at the first write transaction; truncated at commit and rollback; deleted by SQLite only by a hot-journal rollback at open or beside a zero-page main file (§6.3); always inside a mutation lease (SEC-06) |
| Delete rows | `LibraryDatabase` via the T-DELETE constants | Z3 | One published snapshot, one transaction, explicit request (SEC-07) |
| Rename (set aside a Library) | `LibraryStore.QuarantineSet` (`File.Move(overwrite:false)`) | Z3 | Explicit action; SetAside lease; writer lock held; every connection closed first; the member set, main file first (SEC-08, LIB-13) |
| Create the observation spool | `ReportRun.CreateSpool` (`CreateNew`, `FileShare.None`, `DeleteOnClose`) | Z2 | Own name, validated folder, reparse re-check (SEC-27) |
| Delete the observation spool | The OS, when the run's handle closes | Z2 | Delete-on-close only (SEC-27) |
| Launch: open the Library folder | `ReportOpener`, extended allow-list | Z3 | Explicit click; the exact validated Library directory; disabled during observation (SEC-14) |
| Native: 4 new identity queries | `Core/Paths/NativeMethods` | Z1 metadata | Read-only; 0-access handles (§7.8) |
| Hold a 0-access root handle for a capture | `Core/Paths/NativeMethods` (the existing `CreateFileW` with access 0), opened on the enumerated path | Z1 metadata | Share mode read, write and delete; closed when the scan returns (ID-10) |
| Native library: `e_sqlite3.dll` (164 entry points via the provider) | Loaded by the .NET runtime from the single-file extraction directory | process | No resolver; verified at release and at every open (§5.6, SEC-13) |
| Read the drive list for online status | `SourceStatusService` | — | On demand only; local fixed and removable drives only; timeouts (ID-07) |
| Library-change detection | `LibrarySession` (mutation epoch, in memory) and `LibraryStore.ReadHeader` (100-byte read of `library.sqlite3`, full sharing) | Z3 | Read-only; no directory-listing metadata (OBS-04) |
| Report-volume free space for the spool reserve | `Core/Paths/NativeMethods` (`GetDiskFreeSpaceExW`, already listed) | Z2 metadata | Read-only query (SPOOL-24) |

### 16.3 Threats and mitigations

| Threat | Mitigation | ID |
|---|---|---|
| Crafted file names (quotes, `%`, `_`, control characters, unpaired surrogates, very long names) | Always bound as parameters; stored as exact UTF-16 BLOBs; never interpolated into SQL; displayed as text only | SEC-04, SEC-22, NAME-01 |
| SQL injection through the search UI | No user SQL. Filters become parameters, and name matching happens in .NET | SEC-04 |
| Database path substitution: the Library folder or file replaced by a link or junction | Path validation before every open (a reparse point anywhere in the path blocks); a re-check before every `CreateNew`; the header pre-check before SQLite touches a file. **Residual:** a swap in the window between check and open (the same class as v1's folder-swap race). Only the same user or an administrator can write the per-user Library directory, and either could modify the database directly, so `SQLITE_OPEN_NOFOLLOW` adds nothing worth a raw open path (Q-06 resolved) | SEC-02, SEC-32 |
| Source, Library or report aliasing (SUBST, 8.3 names, case, mapped drives) | Canonical-location checks (v1) extended to the Library and the app-data root in both directions (LIB-06) | SEC-26 |
| **Writing inside the tree being observed** (the rejected D-09) | The interlock: no Library mutation overlaps any observation window, in either order (INV-17, OBS-07 to OBS-13); the writer exists only inside mutation leases (OBS-12); capture data in the out-of-source spool (INV-21); the mutation epoch and commit generation (INV-19); app-data root blocked (INV-20). **Residual:** another StorageInventory process's scans (L-OBS1) | SEC-25 |
| Corrupt database | Mapped to Damaged; the member set is never deleted or silently recreated; explicit set-aside | §6.8, D-28 |
| **A journal deleted or separated from its database** (defeated atomic commit, [E-4]) | Leftover-files state; set-aside moves the main file first; creation refused beside a non-empty journal; the committed-state check at open; sealed totals verified at every comparison | LIB-13, D-47 |
| Tampered database: hostile values | `application_id` and WAL-format refusal before SQLite opens it; schema fingerprint (no unexpected triggers, views or indexes); `trusted_schema=OFF`; codes range-checked on read (an unknown code is a data error, not a crash); bounded reads; **no path read from the database is ever launched, opened or used as a filesystem path** | SEC-15, SEC-16, SEC-17 |
| Native DLL planting or search-order hijack of `e_sqlite3.dll`; a planted managed assembly for the provider's by-name lookup (`SQLitePCLRaw.batteries_v2`) | The runtime loads the native DLL from the single-file extraction directory; no custom resolver; `AppContext.BaseDirectory` never used for native loading; the smoke test asserts the loaded module's path and hash, that a DLL planted beside the exe is not loaded, and that a dummy `SQLitePCLRaw.batteries_v2.dll` beside the exe is not loaded (Q-22). **Residual:** a same-user attacker who can write `%TEMP%\.net\…` (documented, as for v1's WPF natives; the host is not known to verify extracted files) | SEC-13, SEC-24, A-18 |
| Dependency supply chain | Exact package IDs and versions; lock-file content hashes; locked mode in CI and Publish; source mapping with exact IDs; pruning checked; package-supplied MSBuild targets recorded | SEC-10, BLD-02, BLD-03 |
| Extension loading (compiled into `e_sqlite3.dll`) | Never enabled; the APIs never appear in source | SEC-17, A-07 |
| SQL-level file creation (`ATTACH`, `VACUUM INTO`) | Neither appears in 1.1.0 | SEC-06, A-05 |
| SQLite temporary files | `temp_store = MEMORY`; no `VACUUM`; query plans with unbounded temporary B-trees are forbidden | SEC-05, A-24 |
| The spool leaks private data | Exclusive handle; delete-on-close; lives only in the user's chosen report folder; network and OneDrive warnings name it; left behind only after power loss, and then reported | SEC-27, SPOOL-05, SPOOL-17 |
| The spool is replaced or altered before import | Exclusive handle held from creation to deletion; capture token in its header; SHA-256 of the physical byte sequence verified by the pass V before any record is used, then structural checks and in-transaction counts (SPOOL-23, IMP-05) | SEC-27, SPOOL-06 |
| Privacy leakage from the Library | Private data (README, Settings); no upload; no network or sync location (LIB-05); deletion is logical and Settings says so | SEC-18 |
| Concurrent instances, sessions or writers | Cross-session lock file; one writer connection; publication in one transaction with verification inside; recovery only under the lock | SEC-19, CONC-01 to CONC-10 |
| Database locking (`SQLITE_BUSY`) | In-process gate; `busy_timeout` backstop; a snapshot failure, never retried in a loop | CONC-06, O16 |
| Identity confusion (clones, swaps or re-pointed letters during a scan) | Clones asked when co-mounted, documented otherwise (L-ID1); end-of-capture re-verification through the held handle and a fresh open of the enumerated path; sources without the minimum evidence cannot be saved; away-and-back re-pointing documented (L-ID2) | ID-05, ID-10, ID-13 |
| Unknown collapsing into Missing | The aligned, case-aware absence rule; a table-driven key; key-based proofs only on `Verified` sources within the verified domain, name-independent proofs elsewhere; the oracle; the world-model test with failing controls | INV-16, D-38, NAME-04, NAME-09, TEST-D5, TEST-D6 |
| Denial of service from a huge Library | Bounded reads everywhere; the comparison list cap (PERF-06); paging | PERF-06 |

### 16.4 How `SecurityAuditTests` must evolve (SEC-30)

No rule is deleted. Each broadened rule is replaced by an **equally narrow, exact** rule, and each rule gets a
**negative self-test**: a violating fixture snippet under `tests/` that the rule must reject (A-21). Text rules scan
every first-party source under `src/` and, for package rules, `tests/` too; reflection rules load every first-party
assembly: Core, History, Library and App.

| # | v1 rule | v1.1 rule |
|---|---|---|
| A-01 | `File_creation_happens_only_in_ReportRun` | `FileMode.CreateNew` only in `ReportRun.cs` and `LibraryStore.cs`; `Directory.CreateDirectory` only in those two files; no overwriting or appending modes anywhere; no `File.Write*`, `Copy`, `Replace` or metadata-setting APIs |
| A-02 | (new) | `FileOptions.DeleteOnClose` only in `ReportRun.cs`, in the spool-creation method, together with `FileShare.None` |
| A-03 | `Readers_only_ever_open_files_for_reading` | `new FileStream(` only in `ReportCsvReader.cs`, `ReportWriters.cs`, `ReportRun.cs` and `LibraryStore.cs`. Outside the create-new calls, every construction uses `FileMode.Open` with `FileAccess.Read` (the lock file additionally `FileShare.None`) |
| A-04 | `Deletes_and_renames_happen_only_in_ReportRun…` | `File.Delete` only in `ReportRun.cs` (the Library never deletes files). `File.Move` only in `ReportRun.cs` (workbook) and `LibraryStore.QuarantineSet` (target names matching `library.damaged-*`), both with `overwrite: false`. No folder deletion or move anywhere |
| A-05 | (new) `Sql_is_constant_and_parameterised` | SQL command text only in `src/StorageInventory.Library/Sql/*.cs` constants; no interpolated or concatenated command text; no `ATTACH`, `DETACH`, `VACUUM`, `load_extension` or `writable_schema`; `journal_mode` only as `TRUNCATE`; no `ORDER BY` on a name `BLOB` column; the only `DELETE` statements are T-DELETE's, each constrained by `snapshot_id`; no `DROP` |
| A-06 | (new) `Library_database_access_only_in_LibraryDatabase` | `SqliteConnection` is constructed only in `LibraryDatabase.cs`; every connection string sets `Mode` explicitly to `ReadWrite` (writer factory only) or `ReadOnly`; `ReadWriteCreate`, `Memory` and shared cache never appear; `Pooling=False` |
| A-07 | (new) `Extensions_are_never_enabled` | `EnableExtensions`, `LoadExtension` and `load_extension` never appear in `src/` |
| A-08 | `The_only_native_calls_are_two_read_only_kernel32_functions` | **First-party:** by reflection over every first-party assembly, exactly six P/Invokes, all `kernel32` (the two v1 functions plus `GetVolumeInformationByHandleW`, `GetFileInformationByHandleEx`, `GetVolumePathNameW`, `GetDiskFreeSpaceExW`); `GetFileInformationByHandleEx` is called only with `FileIdInfo`; `CreateFile` stays at desired access 0 |
| A-09 | (new) | **Third-party:** across the four package assemblies, a `DllImport` target other than framework modules exists only in `SQLitePCLRaw.provider.e_sqlite3`, and only to `e_sqlite3` |
| A-10 | (new) | `NativeLibrary`, `SetDllImportResolver` and `AppContext.BaseDirectory` never appear in `src/`; `SQLitePCL.raw.SetProvider` is called exactly once, in `LibraryDatabase.cs`, before any connection |
| A-11 | `No_package_references_anywhere` | **`Package_references_are_exactly_the_approved_set`:** `<PackageReference>` only in `StorageInventory.Library.csproj`; the (ID, version) set equals §5.5 exactly; each has `PrivateAssets="compile"`; no floating versions; no `GlobalPackageReference`, stray `PackageVersion` or `<Reference HintPath=…>` in `src/` or `tests/`; `packages.lock.json` is present and its package set equals the allow-list; `System.Memory` is absent |
| A-12 | `Core_has_no_UI_process_or_network_dependencies` | Unchanged for Core, plus: Core and History reference no `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite` or `SQLitePCL*`, and History no UI assembly; Library references no UI, process or `System.Net.*` assembly; App has **no compile-time reference** to `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite` or `SQLitePCL*` |
| A-13 | (new) | Core's `InternalsVisibleTo` list is exactly History, Library, Core.Tests, History.Tests, Library.Tests and IntegrationTests; `IScanObserver`, the fan-out and the spool types are not public |
| A-14 | (new) `Library_path_resolution_only_in_App` | `SpecialFolder.LocalApplicationData` appears only in `src/StorageInventory.App/Services/LibraryLocation.cs` |
| A-15 | (new) `Lock_only_in_LibraryStore` | The `FileShare.None` lock open appears only in `LibraryStore.cs`; no named `Mutex`, `Semaphore` or `EventWaitHandle` anywhere |
| A-16 | (new) `Observation_cannot_reach_the_Library` | Core has no reference to the Library assembly (so the fan-out and spool writer cannot reach `LibraryDatabase`); at run time, the interlock refuses every mutation lease while Observing and every observation while a mutation lease is held, the only transitions between the two being a capture's own atomic hand-offs (OBS-07, OBS-10; TEST-W2, TEST-W5) |
| A-17 | `Process_launching_happens_only_in_ReportOpener` | Unchanged location. The allow-list gains only the validated Library directory and still rejects any path that comes from Library data |
| A-18 | `No_network_registry_dynamic_code_or_persistence_APIs` | Unchanged for all first-party source. `IsolatedStorage` and `Properties.Settings` stay forbidden (the Library is the only persistence); the Library assembly's references are also checked by reflection for `System.Net.*`. The one known third-party dynamic load (Microsoft.Data.Sqlite's by-name lookup of `SQLitePCL.Batteries_V2, SQLitePCLRaw.batteries_v2`, [E-1]) is recorded here and covered by TEST-R2's planted-assembly case (Q-22) |
| A-19 | `The_app_never_asks_for_elevation` | Unchanged |
| A-20 | (new) `Native_files_in_publish_are_exactly_expected` | **In CI**, on the publish output: the native files equal v1's WPF natives plus `e_sqlite3.dll`; the release smoke test checks the extracted set and the loaded module (TEST-R2, TEST-R3) |
| A-21 | (new) | Every rule above has a negative self-test |
| A-22 | (new) `Pragmas_are_constants` | Pragmas are set only at open, from constants, and read back: `journal_mode = truncate`, `synchronous = 2` on the writer, `foreign_keys = 1`, `trusted_schema = 0`, `temp_store = 2` |
| A-23 | (new, run time) `Owned_file_set` | After every lifecycle scenario (TEST-L4), the Library directory holds only LIB-02 members (the journal may be absent after a recovery, §6.3), and the report folder holds no spool once the run has ended |
| A-24 | (new, run time) `Bounded_query_plans` | For every SQL constant, `EXPLAIN QUERY PLAN` on a fixture Library shows `USE TEMP B-TREE` only in statements with `LIMIT` (bounded top-N) or on a reviewed exception list |
| A-25 | (new) `Library_mutations_need_a_lease` | By reflection: every method of `LibraryStore` and `LibraryDatabase` that can create, write, rename or open for writing (including the writer-connection factory and every write-transaction entry point) takes a `MutationLease` parameter; `MutationLease` and `ObservationLease` have no public or internal constructor outside the interlock type; the scanner entry points (`ScanAsync`, `RunCapture`) are called in the App only from `ScanSession`, which obtains a lease first. Negative self-test: a fixture method that writes without a lease is rejected |
| A-26 | (new, run time) `SQLite_side_file_lifecycle` | In the lifecycle and crash-recovery tests (TEST-L4, TEST-L6, TEST-L8), every create, delete and rename in the Library directory is recorded by a test-only directory watcher and by a names-only listing taken at every interlock transition through a test hook (names in a directory index change at once; only sizes and times can lag), so each event is attributed to the interlock state in which it happened. Allowed: creates of `library.lock` and `library.sqlite3` by `LibraryStore` and of `library.sqlite3-journal` by SQLite; deletes of `library.sqlite3-journal` only by a hot-journal rollback at open or beside a zero-page main file (§6.3); renames only to `library.damaged-*` targets; **every** such event while the interlock is Mutating, **none** while it is Observing or Idle |

### 16.5 Security requirements index

| ID | Requirement |
|---|---|
| SEC-01 | Core and History stay package-free and have no database reference. SQLite is used only by `StorageInventory.Library` |
| SEC-02 | Library writes happen only under the validated Library directory. The path is validated before every open and re-checked before any file is created |
| SEC-04 | All SQL is constant text with bound parameters. There is no user-supplied SQL and no string-built command text |
| SEC-05 | `temp_store = MEMORY`, no `VACUUM`, and bounded query plans: SQLite writes no temporary file anywhere |
| SEC-06 | The main database file is created only with `FileMode.CreateNew` by `LibraryStore`; SQLite is never opened in a create mode; the only file SQLite itself creates, and the only file it may delete, is `library.sqlite3-journal`, and it deletes it only in the two cases of §6.3 (hot-journal rollback at open; a remnant beside a zero-page main file); StorageInventory's own code deletes no Library file (A-04); no `VACUUM INTO` or `ATTACH` in 1.1.0. Every such operation happens inside a mutation lease (A-26) |
| SEC-07 | Row deletion is exactly one published snapshot per explicit request, in one transaction |
| SEC-08 | The only Library rename is set-aside of the member set (`overwrite: false`, main file first, explicit action), performed only while holding the writer lock and a SetAside lease, after closing every connection of the process (LIB-13) |
| SEC-10 | Package references are exactly the approved set, pinned by lock-file content hashes |
| SEC-12a | First-party native surface: exactly six read-only `kernel32` functions (§7.8) |
| SEC-12b | Third-party native surface: one provider assembly importing one module, `e_sqlite3` |
| SEC-13 | `e_sqlite3.dll` is loaded by the runtime from the single-file extraction directory only, never from the executable's folder; verified by the smoke test, including a planted-DLL negative case |
| SEC-14 | Launches stay on explicit clicks; the allow-list is extended only by the validated Library folder, which is disabled during observation |
| SEC-15 | Data read from the Library is untrusted. It is displayed only, read with bounds, and never launched, opened, or used as a filesystem path |
| SEC-16 | Tamper detection at open (`application_id`, format, version, schema fingerprint, committed state), and range-checked codes on read |
| SEC-17 | `trusted_schema = OFF`, extensions never enabled, and only the expected schema objects |
| SEC-18 | The Library and the spool are private data. The Library is never uploaded or placed on a network or sync location; its path is shown; README's "What StorageInventory writes" names it |
| SEC-19 | Cross-session writer lock, acquired before any member is inspected, created or renamed; one capture at a time; publication and verification in one transaction |
| SEC-22 | Crafted names are stored exactly (UTF-16 BLOB) and never interpreted |
| SEC-24 | Residual: a same-user attacker who can write the single-file extraction folder is out of scope (as in v1) |
| SEC-25 | No Library mutation overlaps any observation window, in either order (the interlock, OBS-07 to OBS-13); capture data only in the out-of-source spool; the mutation epoch and commit generation (INV-19) |
| SEC-26 | Canonical-location alias checks cover the Library and the app-data root in both directions against sources and report folders |
| SEC-27 | The spool is create-new, exclusive, delete-on-close, one of the run's own names, never opened by name and never imported unless sealed by the same live process |
| SEC-30 | Audit rules are replaced only by equally narrow, exact rules, never removed, and each has a negative self-test (§16.4) |
| SEC-32 | SQLite never opens a file that fails the header pre-check (foreign, WAL-format, newer schema) |

## 17. Dependency, build and release

| ID | Area | Change for v1.1 |
|---|---|---|
| BLD-01 | Projects | New `src/StorageInventory.History` (package-free, no UI: identity matching, Library path rules, the comparison engine and oracle, history arithmetic, row-stream interfaces) and `src/StorageInventory.Library` (net10.0-windows, no UI, the SQLite adapter). The App references Core, History and Library. New tests: `tests/StorageInventory.History.Tests` (package-free) and `tests/StorageInventory.Library.Tests`, plus the integration project |
| BLD-02 | Pinning | `RestorePackagesWithLockFile=true`; a committed `packages.lock.json` per project that restores packages; `RestoreLockedMode=true` in CI and `build.ps1 -Target Publish`. Content hashes are in the lock file |
| BLD-03 | `nuget.config` | Package source mapping gains exactly the four ADR package IDs, as exact IDs. `System.Memory` must **not** appear in any lock file (NuGet pruning for net10, §5.5); if it does, C4 **stops** and the ADR is revisited, never silently extended |
| BLD-04 | Repo-local toolchain | Unchanged (SDK 10.0.401). `fetch-tools.ps1` is unchanged: the packages come through NuGet under the lock file |
| BLD-05 | Licences | `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md` gains Microsoft.Data.Sqlite (MIT, .NET Foundation), SQLitePCLRaw (the full Apache-2.0 text; the packages carry no NOTICE file, [E-1]) and SQLite (public-domain dedication). The release assets still include `LICENSE` and the notices file |
| BLD-06 | Reproducibility | **A fresh-clone publish must reproduce the recorded SHA-256** (the INV-15 procedure), in C4 and again in C12. The package bytes and `e_sqlite3.dll`'s PE time stamp are fixed [E-1], so byte-identity is expected (Q-08). If it fails, the gate stops; any irreducible difference is documented in `docs/release.md` byte by byte, never hidden |
| BLD-07 | Single file and native loading | `e_sqlite3.dll` is extracted by the .NET host with the WPF natives and loaded by the runtime's probing; no resolver (§5.6). The release notes list the extracted files and the extraction folder |
| BLD-08 | Size | The exe's size increase is measured and recorded (native DLL 1,978,880 bytes plus managed assemblies, before single-file compression) |
| BLD-09 | CI | Unchanged workflow shape. Restore runs in locked mode. History and Library tests run on the hosted runner (temporary Libraries under `%TEMP%`), including the oracle (TEST-D2) and world-model tests (TEST-D5) and the NTFS part of the Q-14 census. The native file set is checked on the publish output (A-20). Benchmarks stay local |
| BLD-10 | Smoke test | Extended: scan, save, the Library path shown, rescan, compare (expecting the Added and Missing counts of a scripted change), browse offline (the source folder renamed away), the second-instance "In use" message, stop-saving, a scan of a source that contains a fixture Library (report folder elsewhere) that publishes without tripping INV-19, the loaded `e_sqlite3.dll`'s path under `%TEMP%\.net\StorageInventory\` and its SHA-256, a dummy `e_sqlite3.dll` beside the copied exe **not** loaded, a dummy `SQLitePCLRaw.batteries_v2.dll` beside the copied exe **not** loaded (Q-22), New scan unavailable while "Saving to History" is shown (UI-13), and `sqlite_version()` = 3.53.3 shown in About |
| BLD-11 | Clean machine | `e_sqlite3.dll` imports only `KERNEL32.dll`, so no Visual C++ runtime is needed (Q-09 resolved, [E-1]). The smoke test on clean Windows 10 22H2 and Windows 11 VMs remains a C12 release check (TEST-R4) |
| BLD-12 | Versions | App `1.1.0`. `library_info` and `snapshot` record the app and SQLite versions. **v1.0 is unaffected by a Library** (it never reads one). A later app migrates a v1.1 Library forward only (§9.6) |
| BLD-13 | Release artefacts | As in v1.0: the exe, `LICENSE` and `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`, plus the SHA-256 in the release notes |
| BLD-14 | Native set in CI | A-20 runs in CI on every build's publish output, not only in the manual smoke test |
| BLD-15 | Pin rule and engine assertion | The package versions are the lowest SQLitePCLRaw version `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.Core` 10.0.12 requires; upgrades are reviewed changes. Every Library open asserts `sqlite_version() = '3.53.3'` and the recorded `sqlite_source_id()` |
| BLD-16 | Package-supplied build logic | `SQLitePCLRaw.lib.e_sqlite3`'s `buildTransitive` targets are recorded in the ADR (§5.5) and pinned by the lock file |
| BLD-17 | RID-aware lock files | Projects that restore packages set `RuntimeIdentifiers=win-x64` so locked restore and `-r win-x64` publish agree (Q-12). `PackageDownload` of the runtime pack stays outside lock files, as in v1 |
| BLD-18 | Documentation | README's "What StorageInventory writes" and privacy text name the Library and the spool (C5, RX-01, RX-02); README's build and dependency paragraph ("The code references no packages…") is replaced by the exact package and native-library statement of RX-03 (C4, confirmed in C12); `docs/architecture.md` gains the observer, spool and import design and the interlock (C1, C4, C5) and the X-1 correction (C3); `docs/native-security-review.md` gains a v1.1 section with the six first-party calls and the one third-party engine (RX-03, RX-04; C11) |

## 18. Test and verification matrix

### 18.1 Tests

| ID | Area | What | Kind | Suite | Gate |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| TEST-B1 | Baseline | Every v1 unit, integration, parity, UI and smoke test passes unchanged after C1 and C2, and at every later gate | regression | all | C1, C2, all |
| TEST-O1 | Observers | SINK-03 order invariants on every fixture (a recording observer), including sequence numbering and final-only folder emission | property | integration | C1 |
| TEST-O2 | Observers | Report bytes identical with and without an extra isolated observer (hash equality), sorted and unsorted | regression | integration | C1 |
| TEST-O3 | Observers | An isolated observer throwing a class A exception at a random point leaves the reports valid and identical; the observer is faulted; `OutputGuard` is not involved | fault injection | integration | C1, C5 |
| TEST-O4 | Observers | Exception classes: an isolated observer's `OperationCanceledException` with and without the token set; `OutOfMemoryException` and `InsufficientExecutionStackException` rethrown (never isolated) and reported as `Failed (Unexpected)`; a critical `IOException` → `OutputError` | fault injection | Core.Tests | C1 |
| TEST-O5 | Observers | Core's public API surface equals v1's (reflection diff); re-baselined once in C5 to allow exactly the appended `ScanPhase.SavingToHistory` (G0-O07) | reflection | Core.Tests | C1, C5 |
| TEST-O6 | Observers | Scan cancellation latency with the spool writer attached is within v1's bound | timing | integration | C1, C5 |
| TEST-SP1 | Spool | Codec round trip over a memory stream: the read-back sequence of file, error and finalised folder records is exact on every fixture (unpaired surrogates included); trailer counts and totals equal the result; the digest equals SHA-256 of bytes `[0, L − 32)` computed independently; the pass V accepts every written spool | property | Core.Tests | C1 |
| TEST-SP2 | Spool | File lifecycle: create-new, exclusive, delete-on-close, own name, in the report folder; not listed as an incomplete artefact; gone after success, cancellation, failure and spool fault; the tripwire fires when the report folder is reachable inside the source through an unresolved alias | integration | integration | C5 |
| TEST-SP3 | Spool | A capturing child process is killed: no spool remains, the attempt becomes Interrupted at the next open; manual runs on SMB and OneDrive report folders (Q-20) | fault injection | integration / release checklist | C5 |
| TEST-SP4 | Spool | End to end through the live handle (test hook): each corruption class of TEST-SP7, and a wrong capture token → `NotEligible (SpoolInvalid)` at P5; nothing is imported, published or left in the report folder | fault injection | Library.Tests | C5 |
| TEST-SP5 | Spool | Spool write failure (fault injection) → `SpoolWriteFailed`. On a volume sized so that v1's reports fit but reports plus spool do not (G0-M07): the reports complete and are byte-identical to a Save-off run of the same tree; the outcome is `NotEligible (SpoolWriteFailed)` with the "nearly full" reason; the spool released its space (SPOOL-24) | fault injection | integration | C5 |
| TEST-SP6 | Spool | No API accepts a spool path (reflection); a stale spool file in the report folder is never opened | reflection / integration | Library.Tests | C5 |
| TEST-SP7 | Spool | Codec integrity (D-50): over a memory stream, V rejects every one of: one byte changed at each position class (header, file record, error record, inside a run read out of key order, folder section, run index, trailer field, digest); the file truncated at several offsets; bytes appended after the digest; two file records swapped, and a folder's file run split in two, each **with the digest recomputed** (the structure check must catch them); run-index entries reordered with the digest recomputed; an incorrect digest. No record is returned to any caller before V passes | fault injection | Core.Tests | C1 |
| TEST-W1 | Observation | On Windows, the production sequence: Library open (Open lease), T0 (Prepare lease, writer closed after it), window opened, reader connections opening, querying and closing throughout the window, window closed. Asserted: the mutation epoch and commit generation are unchanged; the SHA-256 of every Library member's **contents**, read through handles before and after, is unchanged; no member is created or deleted (names-only listing); repeated read transactions change nothing; no write handle is open during the window (test hook). Recorded as information for Q-21, not asserted: whether the directory listing's sizes and times equal the handles' values (G0-M04) | integration | integration | C4 (reads), C5 |
| TEST-W2 | Observation | The interlock (OBS-07 to OBS-13), as a unit: every (state, request) pair gives the grant or refusal of §11.6 and UI-13; the hand-offs are atomic and only by the same capture; randomised concurrent request sequences from several threads never violate the invariant (checked after every step); test hooks that pause inside **each** mutation kind (Open with hot-journal rollback, Create, Prepare, Save with V, T-IMPORT and T-OUTCOME, Delete, SetAside) show that an observation request is refused while it runs and granted only after it ends, and that no mutation starts while Observing; Faulted refuses everything | unit / concurrency | Library.Tests | C4, C5 |
| TEST-W3 | Observation | During the window: a test-only raw connection commits a change to the Library → `NotEligible (LibraryChangedDuringScan)` through the commit generation; a test hook that advances the epoch → the same through the epoch | fault injection | integration | C5 |
| TEST-W4 | Observation | Sources and report folders equal to or inside `%LOCALAPPDATA%\StorageInventory\` are blocked, literally and through aliases | integration | integration | C5 |
| TEST-W5 | Observation | G0-B01 end to end: capture 1 of a source containing a fixture Library is paused inside T-IMPORT (test hook); a Save-off scan and a Save-on scan of the same source are requested: both are refused with the saving reason and create nothing; after capture 1 commits (and, in a second run, after it rolls back on Stop saving), the scan starts, its window sees an unchanged epoch and commit generation, and capture 1's outcome is recorded before the new T0. ## 19. Implementation gates

Each gate is small, reviewable and merged separately. **No gate relies on unreviewed work from a later gate, and no
gate may choose an architectural contract this specification leaves open:** every prerequisite below is specified here,
and every remaining `OPEN` item has a safe default, an exact experiment (§21) and a stop condition. Documentation
changes in every gate keep `README.md`, `docs/architecture.md` and the security review accurate for what has shipped.

**G0 (before any gate):** the independent implementation-readiness re-review (`35c841d`) required RR-01 to RR-10; this
revision applies them (§22.3). A **focused re-check of RR-01 to RR-09** against the re-review (its §15) must accept this
revision before any gate starts. The re-review noted that C1 and C2 depend on none of the required revisions, so the
owner may authorise them earlier.

### 19.1 Readiness summary

| Gate | Prerequisites (specified in) | All resolved in this spec? | Remaining OPEN (safe default) | Can start after |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| C1 | SINK-01 to SINK-09, CAT-01 to CAT-05, CAN-01a to CAN-01c, SPOOL-12, SPOOL-19 to SPOOL-21, SPOOL-23 (the codec's digest and pass V), D-45, D-46, D-50 (§11) | **Yes** | none | G0 |
| C2 | UI-01, UI-02, UI-12, §14.6 | **Yes** | none | G0 |
| C3 | ID-01 to ID-13, §7.2 (handle on the enumerated path), §7.8, NAME-03 (root suggestions), D-42 | **Yes** | Q-02 (failed call → PathOnly; missing ID-13 items → no saving), Q-13 (root IDs never used for volume identity), Q-19 (hold the handle) | G0 |
| C4 | D-33, D-34, D-36, D-37, D-51, §5.5 to §5.8, LIB-01 to LIB-13 (lock-first creation and set-aside), CONC-01 to CONC-06, CONC-08, CONC-10, OBS-07 to OBS-13 (the interlock in `LibrarySession`), §6.3 side-file lifecycle, §9, §16.4 (including A-25, A-26) | **Yes** | Q-05, Q-07, Q-08, Q-11, Q-12, Q-17, Q-21, Q-22 (each with a default and a stop condition) | C3 |

**Dependency graph:** G0 → C1, C2, C3 and C7 (all four can run in parallel). C3 → C4. C1 + C3 + C4 → C5 → C6.
C2 + C6 + C7 → C8. C6 (+ C7) → C9. C5 to C9 → C10. C4 to C10 → C11 → C12 → C13.

**Changes from `c0c031f`:** the spool codec moved into C1 (it is the observer contract's first real consumer and needs
no persistence); the pure comparison engine (C7) no longer waits for C6, because it works on row streams in
package-free code (D-41); C5's scope changed from direct capture writes to the spool plus post-scan import.

### C1: behaviour-preserving scanner observer fan-out and spool codec
- **Scope:**
  - internal `IScanObserver` replaces `IScanSink`; `OnScanStarted`, `OnFolderFinalised` and `OnScanEnded` are added;
    no discovery callback (SINK-08);
  - the fan-out distinguishes critical from isolated observers; `OutputGuard` wraps only the critical one (SINK-05);
  - the exception classes CAT-01 to CAT-05 and the cancellation stages CAN-01a to CAN-01c;
  - the spool codec (format 1, §11.7 SPOOL-20): writer and reader over a `Stream`, as an isolated observer usable in
    tests, with the append-only digest (SPOOL-12) and the verification pass V (SPOOL-23, D-50); the reader exposes
    key-ordered reads only after V has passed. No spool **file** is created in C1;
  - an internal `InventoryScanner` entry point accepting additional isolated observers, for tests.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes (§11.1 to §11.5, SPOOL-12, SPOOL-19 to SPOOL-21, SPOOL-23). **OPEN:** none.
- **Code:** `Core/Scanning/*`, `Core/InventoryScanner.cs`, `Core/Reports/CsvReportSink.cs`, a new `Core/Spool/*`.
- **Tests:** TEST-B1, TEST-O1 to TEST-O6, TEST-SP1, TEST-SP7; parity against both PowerShell oracles; the v1 audit
  rules unchanged; benchmarks show no regression over 5%.
- **Docs:** `docs/architecture.md` (the observer contract, internal).
- **Non-goals:** spool files, persistence, identity, UI; any public API.
- **Acceptance evidence:** every v1 test passes; report bytes identical on every fixture; the order property holds;
  the exception-class tests pass; Core's public surface equals v1's; the codec round-trips every fixture exactly; the
  release smoke test passes.
- **Stop if:** any parity difference; the report-bytes hash changes; a public API changes; any need arises to emit a
  folder before aggregation.

### C2: behaviour-preserving application-shell decomposition
- **Scope:** a shell window with navigation hosting a Scan page; `SetupView`, `ScanProgressView` and `ResultsView`
  using the existing view models; the scan moved from `MainViewModel` into an app-lifetime `ScanSession` (UI-12); no
  new pages.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes. **OPEN:** none.
- **Code:** `App/*`.
- **Tests:** TEST-B1 (UI), TEST-U4, smoke test.
- **Non-goals:** Library, history or any new page.
- **Acceptance evidence:** v1 UI tests pass unchanged; screenshots differ only in the shell chrome; navigating is not
  yet possible to other pages, but the scan survives re-hosting of its view.
- **Stop if:** any behaviour change, or a new binding error.

### C3: stable volume and source identity (no persistence)
- **Scope:** the four P/Invokes (§7.8); a `VolumeEvidence` capture (Core) that opens a handle on the **enumerated
  path**, reads the ID-13 identity items through it, can be held, and can be taken again through the held handle and
  through a fresh open of the same path (ID-10); identity matching as pure functions in History with an in-memory
  candidate store (§7.5); correcting `docs/architecture.md`'s file-ID paragraph (X-1).
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes. **OPEN:** Q-02, Q-13, Q-19, each answered in this gate by its §21 experiment,
  with its safe default until then.
- **Code:** `Core/Paths/NativeMethods.cs`, a new `Core/Identity/*`, `History/Identity/*`.
- **Tests:** TEST-I1, TEST-I3, TEST-I5 (unit and SUBST), manual TEST-I2, TEST-I4 and TEST-I6 where media is available;
  A-08 updated to the six P/Invokes.
- **Docs:** the security review's native-API inventory; §21 updated with the Q-02, Q-13 and Q-19 results.
- **Non-goals:** a database, UI, per-file IDs.
- **Acceptance evidence:** evidence captured with 0-access handles on NTFS; the matching table passes, including the
  clone limitation case; the re-verification opens only the enumerated path (the held handle for E1 and E2, a fresh open for E3) and detects a re-pointed SUBST
  letter and a renamed source folder; Q-02, Q-13 and Q-19 are resolved or explicitly scoped with their evidence
  recorded, including which source types provide the ID-13 items.
- **Stop if:** any identity call needs more than 0 access on some filesystem, or holding the root handle has a
  user-visible effect beyond blocking "safely remove" during a scan (a design review is needed first).

### C4: the Inventory Library and persistent store (SQLite adapter)
- **Scope:** `src/StorageInventory.Library` with the ADR packages (`PrivateAssets="compile"`), lock files and nuget
  mapping; `LibraryStore` (lock-first creation, header pre-check and header generation read, set-aside under the
  lock, writer lock), `LibraryDatabase` (connections, the writer only inside mutation leases, pragmas, engine
  assertion), `LibrarySession` (lock, gate, the **Library activity interlock** with its leases, epoch, hand-offs and
  Faulted state, state value); schema 1, its fingerprint and the §6.8 state machine; recovery at open (T-RECOVER); the one-transaction import primitive over an abstract row
  stream (without the spool); snapshot deletion (T-DELETE); `StorageInventory.History` with the Library path rules;
  notices; the audit rules of §16.4 that concern packages, files, SQL, connections, natives, locking and pragmas.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes (§5, §6, §9, §11.6, §16.4). **OPEN, resolved inside C4:** Q-05 (default: blocked
  with an explanation), Q-07 (measure; integrity does not depend on it), Q-08, Q-11, Q-12, Q-17, Q-21, Q-22 (each by
  its §21 experiment).
- **Code:** a new project; `nuget.config`; `Directory.Build.props` (lock-file properties); `THIRD-PARTY-NOTICES.md`.
- **Tests:** TEST-L1 to TEST-L8, TEST-K1, TEST-S1, TEST-S2, TEST-T4, TEST-C1, TEST-W1 (the read part), TEST-W2 (the
  interlock), TEST-P1, TEST-R1, TEST-R2 (loading part), TEST-R3, TEST-R5, TEST-A1 (the rules above, A-25 and A-26
  included).
- **Docs:** `docs/release.md` (packages, size, extraction, loaded-module check).
- **Non-goals:** capture, spool files, comparison, UI.
- **Acceptance evidence:** the build is reproducible (or the difference is documented and reviewed); the audit is
  green with the new exact rules and their negative self-tests; `sqlite_version() = 3.53.3`; the lock files hold
  exactly the four packages; `e_sqlite3.dll` loads from the extraction directory and a planted copy beside the exe is
  not loaded, nor a planted `SQLitePCLRaw.batteries_v2.dll`; read transactions change no file's contents on Windows;
  the interlock passes TEST-W2, including its concurrency runs; two processes racing to create a Library never produce
  Leftover files; the engine benchmark meets PERF-01 and PERF-15; `SQLITE_FULL` during an import rolls back without
  damage.
- **Stop if:** an unapproved package is pulled in (including `System.Memory`); a native load happens from anywhere but
  the extraction directory, or a planted managed assembly is loaded (Q-22); reproducibility breaks without an
  explanation; TEST-W1 shows a read writing a file; the header read of OBS-04 conflicts with SQLite's share modes on
  Windows; the §15.4 C4 stop condition fires.

### C5: capture through the observation spool and atomic import
- **Scope:** the spool file lifecycle in `ReportRun` (own name, create-new, exclusive, delete-on-close); the
  `InventoryRun` orchestrator (P0 to P7) on the interlock's leases and hand-offs: preflight integration with the
  Library path rules and ID-13, the identity prompt, T0, the observation window with the mutation epoch and commit
  generation, the held root handle and re-verification through the enumerated path, eligibility with the pass V,
  T-IMPORT with in-transaction verification, T-OUTCOME, the save token; the spool's space reserve (SPOOL-24); recovery
  of interrupted attempts at open (from C4) exercised end to end.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes (§10, §11.6 to §11.8). **OPEN:** Q-18 (measure the spool and confirm the reserve
  factor), Q-20 (confirm delete-on-close on SMB, OneDrive and with antivirus), both answered in this gate.
- **Code:** `Core/Reports/ReportRun.cs` (spool name and creation), `Library/Capture/*`, `History/PathRules/*`.
- **Tests:** TEST-SP2 to TEST-SP6, TEST-W1 to TEST-W5, TEST-I5 (end to end), TEST-K1 (second-instance scan),
  TEST-K2, TEST-S3, TEST-S4, TEST-T1 to TEST-T3, TEST-T5, TEST-T6, TEST-L4, TEST-O3 (with the real spool writer),
  TEST-O5 (re-baselined for `SavingToHistory`), TEST-P2, TEST-A1 (A-02, A-16, A-23, A-25).
- **Docs:** README's "What StorageInventory writes" and privacy text; `docs/architecture.md` (spool and import).
- **Non-goals:** comparison, history UI.
- **Acceptance evidence:** every O-row of §10.4 is tested; nothing is visible before `COMMIT`; older snapshots are
  unchanged under every failure; the Library directory is unchanged during observation, including a
  Library-inside-source fixture; no spool remains after any end except a simulated power loss; PERF-02a, PERF-02b,
  PERF-03, PERF-11a, PERF-11b, PERF-13, PERF-14 and PERF-16 are met or revised with measured justification.
- **Stop if:** any path publishes after a scan that did not finish; any Library mutation overlaps an observation
  window in either order; any capture modifies another snapshot; a spool survives an ordinary end.

### C6: history and query services
- **Scope:** the catalogue (`PublishedSnapshotId`), capture-order timelines and attempts; `LibraryExplorer` (§13.4);
  the row streams that feed the comparison engine; snapshot deletion through the UI service; Library size.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes. **OPEN:** none.
- **Tests:** TEST-H1, TEST-H2, TEST-H3, TEST-S5, TEST-K3, TEST-T3, TEST-W5 (the paused-deletion case); PERF-07,
  PERF-08, PERF-10 (from TEST-P3's scenarios).
- **Non-goals:** the comparison engine, search.
- **Acceptance evidence:** `LibraryExplorer` has parity with `ReportExplorer`, including ties, in sorted and unsorted
  mode; deletion is one scoped transaction; capture order holds after deletions.

### C7: the comparison engine (package-free)
- **Scope:** §12 in full, in `StorageInventory.History`: alignment, the case-aware absence proof, two-axis folders,
  DIFF-05, input verification, the summary, top-N lists, the capped item list, flags; the reference oracle and the
  world-model generator; the `K` function and its generated Unicode 16.0.0 tables, the `Dom` predicate, the matching
  assurance and the verified-class list (NAME-03, NAME-04, NAME-09).
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes. **OPEN:** Q-03 (default: exFAT inside DIFF-05) and Q-14 (default: every class
  `Unverified`; the NTFS census runs in CI, exFAT, FAT32 and ReFS manually), answered or scoped in this gate.
- **Tests:** TEST-D1, TEST-D2, TEST-D3, TEST-D5, TEST-D6, TEST-D8; PERF-05, PERF-06 and PERF-12 on synthetic 2M row
  streams.
- **Non-goals:** UI; move detection; the Library-backed row streams (C6).
- **Acceptance evidence:** T1 to T53 pass; each property passes ≥ 10,000 generated cases; the world-model test finds
  **zero** unsound verdicts in ≥ 200,000 worlds per assurance level and both of its controls find some; no Missing
  appears under an unobservable anchor in any test; every census class lies within one `K` class; the verified-class
  list contains exactly the classes whose Q-14 evidence passed; the synthetic budgets are met.
- **Stop if:** any unsound verdict in TEST-D5; any case where Unknown and Missing cannot be told apart from stored data
  (a schema question: return to review before C4's schema is changed); the NTFS census cannot verify NTFS (Missing on
  the most common source would then always need an empty folder: a product question for review, not a code change).

### C8: History, Compare, Sources and Home UI
- **Scope:** the pages of §14.1 except Search; the Scan page's "Save to History" option, preflight line and saving

- **Acceptance evidence:** the **first vertical slice** (scan → save → scan again → compare) works end to end through

- **Scope:** SREQ-01 (search), SREQ-02 (extension growth) and SREQ-03 (age cohorts), each separately shippable.
- **Prerequisites resolved:** yes. **OPEN:** none.

- **Scope:** the §15.4 scale plan; `docs/benchmarks/library.md`; fixes needed to meet PERF budgets. It does **not**

## 20. Decision register

History is kept: superseded decisions stay listed with the decision that replaces them. "Revised" means the decision
stands with the stated change. Every `DECIDED` entry is subject to G0.

| ID | Decision | Status | Basis |
|---|---|---|---|
| D-01 | Persistence: SQLite through `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.Core` 10.0.12 with SQLitePCLRaw 2.1.12, provider set explicitly, native DLL "loaded by full path from `AppContext.BaseDirectory`" | **SUPERSEDED by D-33** (SQLite retained; the load path was wrong) | REV-H01, REV-O01 to REV-O04 |
| D-02 | The SQLite dependency is isolated in `StorageInventory.Library`; Core stays package-free | **SUPERSEDED by D-41** | REV-M13 |
| D-03 | Engine settings: WAL, `NORMAL` for staging and `FULL` for publish, `trusted_schema=OFF`, `temp_store=MEMORY`, no extensions, `foreign_keys=ON` | **SUPERSEDED by D-37** | REV-M01, REV-M08, REV-H02, REV-B01, [E-2] |
| D-04 | The package policy becomes an exact allow-list plus lock-file hashes; the audit rule is replaced, never removed | DECIDED (revised: A-09 to A-11, A-20, A-21 also cover other reference mechanisms, RID-aware lock files, third-party natives and CI) | REV-H06, REV-O13 |
| D-05 | Both Complete and Incomplete finished scans are publishable snapshots | DECIDED (unchanged; completeness evidence LIFE-09) | review: ACCEPT |
| D-06 | Only a scan with `Finished = true` can publish. A report failure means no snapshot | DECIDED (reaffirmed explicitly as LIFE-08, with reasons) | REV-O09 |
| D-07 | Full snapshots with shared name and path dictionaries; no delta storage in v1.1 | DECIDED (revised: measured and projected evidence separated; CAP-01 to CAP-05 replace PERF-09) | REV-O08 |
| D-08 | Library location: a fixed `%LOCALAPPDATA%\StorageInventory\Library\` | **SUPERSEDED by D-34** | review: ACCEPT WITH REVISION |
| D-09 | The Library may lie inside a scanned source; it is written during the scan and scanned as ordinary metadata | **REJECTED; SUPERSEDED by D-35** | REV-B01 |
| D-10 | Assembly boundaries: Core (scan, identity), Library (persistence, comparison, history), App (UI) | **SUPERSEDED by D-41** | REV-M13 |
| D-11 | Observers: reports are critical, the snapshot observer is isolated; a snapshot failure never invalidates reports | DECIDED (revised: the isolated observer is the spool writer; `OutputGuard` wraps only the critical observer; exception classes D-45; no channel or writer thread during traversal) | REV-M03 |
| D-12 | Cancelled, failed and interrupted attempts are `scan_attempt` rows, never snapshots | DECIDED (revised: outcomes NotEligible and SaveCancelled; session and capture tokens; T0 before observation) | REV-M07, REV-H03 |
| D-13 | Changed = size or modified time differs; created, attributes and name spelling are informational; last access is ignored | DECIDED (revised: the FAT clock-shift rule is D-40; "Changed" is scoped to listing metadata) | REV-M05, REV-O06 |
| D-14 | Comparison results are computed on demand and never stored | DECIDED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-15 | Move and rename detection: deferred | DEFERRED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-16 | No per-file ID column in schema 1 | DECIDED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-17 | Hard links: each path is an observation (v1 semantics) | CONFIRMED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-18 | Item identity uses exact-case names; case-only renames match through the constrained fallback | **SUPERSEDED by D-38** | REV-B02 |
| D-19 | The PowerShell reference remains the oracle for traversal and reports only; persistence is not reimplemented in PowerShell | DECIDED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-20 | Folder deltas are ranked only when Exact | **SUPERSEDED by D-39** (the rule is kept inside a two-axis model) | REV-H05 |
| D-21 | Volume identity levels (Strong, Moderate, PathOnly); never silent merges; ask when ambiguous | **SUPERSEDED by D-42** | REV-H04, REV-M11 |
| D-22 | Network sources are identified by the canonical share path only; no name resolution | DECIDED (revised: "compared by location" wording; server and share names matched through `K`) | review: ACCEPT WITH REVISION |
| D-23 | Concurrency: a named `Local\` mutex per Library; one capture at a time | **SUPERSEDED by D-36** | REV-H03 |
| D-24 | Snapshot-only scans are SHOULD, gated on the results screen reading from the Library | **SUPERSEDED by D-43** (moved to 1.2) | review §16B |
| D-25 | Search is a filter model over a single snapshot | DECIDED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-26 | Automatic retention: deferred | DEFERRED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-27 | Scheduled or background scans, the USN journal and watchers: deferred | DEFERRED | review: ACCEPT |
| D-28 | Library corruption never leads to deletion or silent recreation; set-aside only on explicit action | DECIDED (revised: the member set is set aside, main file first; the Leftover files state; "Keep a copy" dropped) | REV-H02, [E-4] |
| D-29 | Schema migrations are forward-only, backup first, one transaction per step; newer schemas are never opened | DECIDED (revised: backups created create-new under a `.partial` name; not exercised in 1.1.0) | REV-M02 |
| D-30 | Filesystem-derived strings are stored as exact UTF-16 BLOBs | DECIDED (revised justification: NAME-08) | REV-O10 |
| D-31 | Release packaging: the same shape as v1.0; reproducibility re-verified | DECIDED (revised: native loading per D-33; CI native file check) | REV-H01, REV-H06 |
| D-32 | Online status is probed only for local fixed and removable drives, never network or optical | DECIDED (revised: **on demand only** in 1.1.0; tested by TEST-I7) | REV-M12 |
| **D-33** | **Persistence ADR (§5):** SQLite retained, through `Microsoft.Data.Sqlite.Core` 10.0.12 and SQLitePCLRaw 2.1.12 `core`, `provider.e_sqlite3`, `lib.e_sqlite3` (SQLite 3.53.3), provider set explicitly; **no custom native resolver**: the runtime loads `e_sqlite3.dll` from the single-file extraction directory; the loaded module is verified at release and the engine version at every open; the pin rule of BLD-15 | DECIDED | Supersedes D-01; [E-1] |
| **D-34** | **Library location:** fixed `%LOCALAPPDATA%\StorageInventory\Library\` in 1.1; the app-data root can be neither a source nor a report folder; the Library may lie inside other sources **only under D-35** | DECIDED | Supersedes D-08 |
| **D-35** | **No Library mutation while observing:** within the process, no Library mutation overlaps an observation window, in either order (every scan; enforced by D-51); capture data goes to an out-of-source spool in the report folder; the snapshot is imported in one transaction only after the scan has finished; the Library folder is observed, not excluded, and labelled; a Library-mutation tripwire based on the interlock's mutation epoch and the database's commit generation, never on directory-listing metadata; another process's scans are limitation L-OBS1; the spool yields space to the reports (SPOOL-24) | DECIDED (revised after G0: G0-B01, G0-M01, G0-M04, G0-M07) | Supersedes D-09; REV-B01, review §12.4; re-review §5 ACCEPT WITH REVISION; [E-8] |
| **D-36** | **Concurrency:** an exclusively held lock file (cross-session), acquired before any member is inspected, created or renamed and held until the process exits; creation and set-aside only under it; one writer connection, open only inside a mutation lease; import, verification and publication in one `BEGIN IMMEDIATE` transaction; recovery only under the lock; an in-process reader/writer gate; no named kernel object | DECIDED (revised after G0: G0-M06; window ordering by D-51) | Supersedes D-23; REV-H03; re-review §5 ACCEPT WITH REVISION |
| **D-37** | **Engine configuration (§5.8):** rollback journal `TRUNCATE`, `synchronous=FULL` for every write, `temp_store=MEMORY`, no `VACUUM`, `VACUUM INTO` or `ATTACH` in 1.1.0, `foreign_keys=ON`, `trusted_schema=OFF`, extensions never enabled, `busy_timeout` as a backstop | DECIDED | Supersedes D-03; [E-2], [E-3], [E-5] |
| **D-38** | **Names and absence:** exact stored names; a table-driven candidate key `K(n) = R(NFD(R(n)))` from Unicode 16.0.0 data, with no OS or runtime casing or normalisation API; a per-source matching assurance (`Verified` only for filesystem classes whose Q-14 evidence passed; every network source `Unverified`) and a verified domain; case-fold matching only under two Listed parents and singleton key classes; Missing (Added) on `Verified` sources only when the Listed counterpart has no same-type child with the same key and every name concerned is in the verified domain, and on `Unverified` sources only when the exactly aligned Listed counterpart holds nothing of that type; otherwise Unknown | DECIDED (revised after G0: G0-M02) | Supersedes D-18; REV-B02, [E-6] (rule), [E-9] (key); re-review §5 ACCEPT WITH REVISION |
| **D-39** | **Folders on two axes:** an existence class (Added, Missing, Present, Unknown) and a separate content certainty (Exact, NotComparable, NotObserved); only Exact deltas are ranked | DECIDED | Supersedes D-20; REV-H05 |
| **D-40** | **FAT-family clock shifts:** on FAT, FAT32 and exFAT, equal size and a non-zero whole quarter-hour offset within ±14 h is Unknown (ChangeIndeterminate), never Unchanged | DECIDED | Revises D-13; REV-M05 |
| **D-41** | **Assembly boundaries:** Core (scan, observers, spool codec, identity evidence; package-free), History (identity matching, path rules, comparison, history arithmetic, oracle; package-free), Library (the SQLite adapter only, packages with `PrivateAssets="compile"`), App (UI, no compile-time SQLite reference) | DECIDED | Supersedes D-02, D-10; REV-M13 |
| **D-42** | **Identity model (§7):** six separate concepts; confidence and basis fixed per source; exact-case root key with case-variant suggestions only; clones indistinguishable unless co-mounted (documented); folder-mounted volumes are not sources; end-of-capture re-verification through a root handle opened on **the enumerated path** and held (E1, E2) and a fresh open of **the enumerated path** (E3), never of the canonical path; a minimum evidence set per source type, without which the source cannot be saved (ID-13); away-and-back re-pointing documented (L-ID2) | DECIDED (revised after G0: G0-M03) | Supersedes D-21; REV-H04, REV-M11; re-review §5 ACCEPT WITH REVISION |
| **D-43** | **Scope moves:** SREQ-04 and SREQ-08 to 1.2; SREQ-05, SREQ-06, SREQ-07, SREQ-09 and SREQ-10 to 1.1.x; the totals chart is SHOULD (SREQ-11); "Keep a copy" dropped; the automatic online sweep to 1.1.x | DECIDED | Supersedes D-24; review §16B |
| **D-44** | **Order:** snapshots are ordered by capture sequence; `snapshot_id` is never reused; clock anomalies are flagged | DECIDED | REV-M04 |
| **D-45** | **Exception classes** A to D (§11.4) | DECIDED | REV-M03, X-6 |
| **D-46** | **Visibility:** the observer interface, fan-out and spool types stay internal until the 1.2 integration design; no discovery callback in 1.1 | DECIDED | REV-M13 |
| **D-47** | **Sealed totals:** verified inside T-IMPORT and at every comparison; the committed-state check at every open | DECIDED | [E-4] |
| **D-48** | **Discovery ordinals** (`seq`, `discovery_index`) are stored so that browsing matches v1's tie order | DECIDED | REV-M09 |
| **D-49** | **Spool deletion** only through the delete-on-close handle of the creating run; stale spools are never imported or deleted automatically | DECIDED | REV-B01 (follow-up) |
| **D-50** | **Spool integrity:** the spool is append-only; one SHA-256 covers every byte before the digest, in physical order; a sequential verification pass V (digest, structure, rebuilt run index, totals) runs at P5 before any record is used, and key-ordered reads happen only after it; format 1 is unchanged; per-section digests are not adopted | DECIDED | G0-M05; decided before C1 builds the codec |
| **D-51** | **Library activity interlock:** one app-lifetime state machine in `LibrarySession` (Idle, Mutating, Observing, Faulted) whose leases every Library mutation and every observation window must hold; the two kinds exclude each other in both directions; a capture's leases are joined by atomic hand-offs, so the next scan becomes eligible only when the previous capture's outcome is recorded; refusals rather than queues; the writer connection exists only inside mutation leases; command availability is derived from the interlock state | DECIDED | G0-B01, G0-M04; re-review RR-01 |

## 21. Open questions

Every question the review answered with evidence is closed here, citing it. The rest stay `OPEN` with a safe default,
the exact experiment that answers them, and the earliest gate that must resolve them. **None lets a gate choose an
architectural contract:** each default is already the specified behaviour, and each experiment can only confirm it or
trigger a stop condition that sends the question back to review.

| ID | Question | Status | Evidence or reason | Safe default | Exact experiment | Resolve by |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|

| Q-02 | Do `GetVolumeInformationByHandleW`, `FileIdInfo` and `GetFinalPathNameByHandleW` work with 0-access handles on FAT32, exFAT, UDF, SMB (a Windows server and a Samba server) and a mapped drive, and what do they return when they succeed (for example, is the 64-bit serial the 32-bit one zero-extended on FAT)? | OPEN | Only NTFS was probed; the review had no Windows | A failed call gives `PathOnly` with the reason; confidence is keyed by filesystem name, so FAT never reaches Strong; a source lacking an ID-13 item cannot be saved (reports only) | On Windows 10 and 11: a USB stick or VHDX formatted FAT32 and exFAT, a mounted ISO (UDF; Explorer's Mount needs no admin), SMB shares and a mapped letter. Open each root, **through the path as entered**, with access 0 and backup semantics, call the four functions and `GetFinalPathNameByHandleW`, record success, values and errors, and which ID-13 items each type provides | C3 |
| Q-03 | exFAT timestamps across time-zone and DST changes | OPEN | Not observed on real media | exFAT is inside DIFF-05 (Unknown, never Unchanged) | Files written to exFAT by Windows and, if available, by a camera or phone; change the time zone (+5:30, −3:00) and DST; rescan; record the `LastWriteTimeUtc` shifts. Only if neither source ever shifts may exFAT leave the rule, by reviewed change | C7 |

Every pair the volume treats as one name must have equal `K`. **(4) Case-sensitive directories:** repeat (2) and (3) in a directory marked case-sensitive where the runner permits; every name must be distinct. NTFS in CI (no admin); exFAT, FAT32 and ReFS (Dev Drive) manually. A class that fails any part stays `Unverified`; widening `K` instead is a reviewed change | C7 |

The review (`docs/v1.1-preproduction-review.md` at `d7f91bf`) was treated as evidence, not authority. Each finding was
located in the `c0c031f` text, checked against the repository and primary sources ([E-1] to [E-7]), and repaired in the
authoritative sections listed. Where the repair departed from the review's proposal, the reason and the evidence are
given. No finding is left open and none was rejected. Where a later correction (§22.3) changed a repaired rule, the
tables below still describe the repair of `456b233`; the current rule is the one in the authoritative section.

### 22.3 G0 re-review dispositions (G0-B01 to G0-O10, RR-01 to RR-10)

The re-review (`docs/v1.1-preproduction-rereview.md` at `35c841d`, not in this tree, G0-O01) was treated as evidence,
not authority, and each finding was re-derived against this text and v1's code before it was repaired. Its §15
numbering (RR-01 to RR-10) is used throughout this document. The correction brief for this revision listed the same
work under a different numbering; the last column of the RR table maps it, so that nothing is skipped under either
numbering.

**How G0-B01 is closed.** The failure needed a scan's window to open while a Library write was already running
(G0-B01 modes 1 and 3), or a capture's import to be refused because a later scan's window had opened (mode 2). Now:
(1) every Library mutation, StorageInventory's or SQLite's on its behalf, happens only inside a mutation lease
(OBS-08), and the type rule A-25 makes a mutation without a lease impossible to write; (2) a window opens only from
Idle or by a capture's own atomic hand-off, and a mutation lease is granted only from Idle or by a capture's own
hand-off (OBS-07), both decided in one monitor section (OBS-09), so neither order of overlap can occur; (3) a capture
holds the interlock from T0 to its recorded outcome through hand-offs (OBS-10), so no scan can start while a capture
saves, and no capture can lose its import or outcome to a later scan; (4) the UI derives every command from the same
state (UI-13), and a request that races a state change is refused with nothing started (OBS-11, O28, O29); (5) a
defect in any of this would still be caught before publication by the mutation epoch and the commit generation
(INV-19, OBS-04). The failing sequence of the re-review (import running, New scan of `C:\`) is TEST-W5, which also
pauses a deletion, an open-time recovery and a set-aside. The cross-instance case is out of one process's control and
is stated as L-OBS1 (G0-M01).

**Findings.**

| Finding | Confirmed? | Disposition | Where it is repaired | Departure from the re-review's proposal, with reason |
|---|---|---|---|---|

### Verdict

| No new fundamental architecture decision remains open | Yes: D-50 and D-51 are decided here; the accepted architecture (SQLite, rollback `TRUNCATE`, `%LOCALAPPDATA%` Library, out-of-source spool, post-observation atomic import, exact names, strict Missing/Unknown, single-writer ownership, internal observer API, full snapshots, the C1 to C13 structure) is unchanged |
| C1 requires no implementation-time invention | Yes |
| C4 and C5 are blocked only by scheduled platform verification, not by missing design | Yes: Q-08, Q-11, Q-12, Q-17, Q-21, Q-22 (C4); Q-18, Q-20 (C5) |

## `READY FOR FOCUSED G0 RE-CHECK`

**Conditions attached to the verdict:**

1. This is a readiness verdict for the **focused G0 re-check of RR-01 to RR-09** that the re-review names, not
   implementation authority. No gate starts before that re-check accepts this revision (C1 and C2 may be authorised
   earlier by the owner, as the re-review allows).
2. After G0, C1, C2, C3 and C7 may start in parallel; the others follow §19.1.
3. No v1.1 implementation exists. This revision changes no code, test, build file, package reference, existing v1
   document or review document; it changes only this file and appends E-8 and E-9 to the non-normative
   `docs/v1.1-preproduction-repair-evidence.md`.
